# Replan

`dt status` says `replan`: something learned during the build, or a major bug (after `pipeline-design` or `architecture-design` Revise, if a step or decision changed). Example: `examples-replan.md`.

1. Take `blocked_tasks`, the docs under `changed_since_plan` and their revised sections (`dt section <ID>`). Until those docs are re-approved, `dt status` says `blocked`: wait. Reorder, split or add; new IDs from `dt next-id M<n>-T`. Never renumber or reuse an ID. A new or split task meets `tasks.md`; a step a revised pipeline adds gets a task or a Deferred entry by name (`cut.md` step 3). Each changed `Depends on` changes its `## Dependency graph` row; a frozen interface that must change is a `contract-change`: a new interface task before every task that needs it (`phase0.md`, Frozen means frozen).
2. **Never drop an unchecked task silently.** List each drop with its reason for approval. On approval, remove it from plan, graph and todo and keep a retired line under Replan notes: `- ~~M2-T05: Title~~ Retired YYYY-MM-DD: reason`.
3. Remove a `**Blocked:**` line only when the change resolves it. A major bug's regression case becomes an acceptance bullet.
4. Add one dated line to `## Replan notes`, set Status to Draft and update `Last updated` (the plan is then newer than the revised docs, which clears `changed_since_plan`), run the Review Gate. Never touch a checked task.

# Example: Replan — the spectrum needs the Fourier fit first

**State:** mid-M1 (the plan of `examples-phase0.md`). The build paused M1-T11:
```markdown
**Blocked:** TC-02 fails on the hour-of-week means profile when T < 5 weeks; the spectrum needs the Fourier profile as input
```
```
mode: replan
blocked_tasks: M1-T11
```

No step changed (PL-02 already runs `fit_seasonality` before `spectrum`), so no `pipeline-design` Revise is needed; only the graph changes. Had a step changed, `pipeline-design` Revise would run first: while `pipelines.md` is Draft, `dt status` says `blocked` (`waiting for docs/packages/gridload/pipelines.md approval`); once it's re-approved with a later `Last updated` than the plan, it says `replan` with `changed_since_plan: docs/packages/gridload/pipelines.md`.

**Edits:**
- Move the M1-T12 section above M1-T11. IDs stay; plan position sets the order, and `dt lint` checks `Depends on` by position.
- M1-T11: `**Depends on:** M1-T05, M1-T12`; remove the `Blocked` line. Its `Writes:` and M1-T12's stay disjoint.
- The graph row that let them run side by side, `| M1-T11 LS amplitudes | M1-T12 Fourier seasonality | data | profile.Profile sample (M1-T01): … |`, becomes `| M1-T11 LS amplitudes | M1-T12 Fourier seasonality | order | none: TC-02 needs the Fourier profile as the spectrum's input |`. The critical path grows from 4 to 5 (M1-T01 → M1-T04 → M1-T12 → M1-T11 → M1-T14).
- M1-T11 gains no new bullet: TC-02 already covers it.
- Reorder `todo.md` to match.
- `## Replan notes`: `- 2026-10-28: M1-T11 now waits on M1-T12 (the spectrum needs the Fourier profile; TC-02 failed on hour-of-week means); dropped none`
- Status → Draft.

**Gate message:** what moved and why, the new edge and the longer critical path, "dropped none". *(Turn ends.)*

**A drop looks like this.** It's listed for approval first ("Drop M1-T13? Seasonal removal is already checked by TC-02 and TC-03 in M1-T14's run. Default: keep"). Only after an unambiguous yes, remove the section, its graph rows and todo line, and add under Replan notes:
```markdown
- ~~M1-T13: Seasonal removal check~~ Retired 2026-10-28: TC-02 and TC-03 now run in M1-T14
```
`dt next-id` counts the retired ID, so it's never reused, and `dt lint` flags any `Depends on` still pointing at it.
# Extend

`dt status` says `extend`: a stage is newly designed (`stage_to_cut`), or the next milestone needs a plan. Example: `examples-routing.md` (a milestone with stage skills and references).

1. **Grow check, then cut the stage:** `cut.md` steps 1–2. A designed stage with no rows is cut now, rows only, even while an earlier milestone waits for its plan.
2. **Plan the next milestone at kickoff**, in `tasks/queued/` only if the user asks; never merge it into the active plan. Scope it (`cut.md` step 3), shape it (`phase0.md`), then write its tasks and files (`tasks.md`).
3. Run the Review Gate.
# Grow check, cutting a stage, scoping a milestone

Read before planning a milestone, before any task is written. Example: `examples-cut.md` (a large stage cut into slices).

Read only `dt status`, the roadmap, the packages' `pipelines.md`, `contract.md` headings and `tests.md`, the spec sections they cover, the skill list and the `milestone_references` entries.

## 1. Grow check

Before planning, check the Grow triggers. `grow_triggers` lists the measurable ones (> 3 pipelines, > 12 modules, package docs > 2,000 lines). Check the others yourself:
- The milestone brings a dependency stack the rest shouldn't carry (torch, CUDA).
- A part runs or deploys on its own (a paper-trading service).

If one fires and no `AD-NN` already records the decision to stay single, **stop** and route to `architecture-design` Grow. Plan after Grow, pipeline-design Grow and build-setup Extend have caught up.

## 2. Cut the stage into milestones

When `stage_to_cut` names a stage, its pipelines are `pipelines_inferred`: the ones covering the stage's spec IDs. When the brief lists every stage, each stage's Source is `brief` and the spec's only Changelog row says IDs `all`, so `dt` can't tell which pipelines belong to which stage and `pipelines_inferred` comes back empty: assign each approved pipeline to the stage whose outcome it delivers, by its Covers.

- **Order by dependency.** Which pipeline needs another's outputs? A consumer needs its provider's *interface*, not its finished build: a boundary the interface task freezes lets both sides build in the same milestone.
- **The first milestone is a walking skeleton:** the thinnest end-to-end path through every package the stage touches, one pipeline per package at spine version, joined across each boundary, so the user can run it end to end.
- **Each later milestone is a vertical slice:** it adds a capability the user can run end to end (categories, transfers, the review screen), thickening the skeleton. Never a horizontal layer ("all of the relay", "every model"), which can't run on its own, and never a leftover of what didn't fit: viewers, reference checks and write-ups go in the milestone that builds what they show, and that milestone's exit holds the stage's quality bar (the snake beats both baselines).
- **Cut along dependency lines,** only where a later milestone needs an earlier one's outputs, and name them in the row's `Depends`: `M1 (profile.Profile, PL-01 output)`. Depends lists only the milestones whose outputs this one needs, never a build order or a note naming another milestone: `dt` reads every `M<n>` in the cell as an edge. Independent tracks stay in one milestone as parallel waves: one milestone is active at a time, so splitting them only serializes them.
- **Size each one** against the cap (step 3): a critical path of at most about 15 tasks. Cut until each fits; fewer, larger milestones beat many thin ones. A stage that fits one milestone gets one.
- **Every pipeline lands** in the milestone that first wires it; a later slice that thickens it lists it again. Every spec ID of the stage lands in a milestone or is Deferred later.

Write each milestone, IDs from `dt next-id M` (write the row before asking again). If the roadmap has no milestone table, add it under the stage table:

```markdown
| Milestone | Stage | Name | Packages | Pipelines | Depends | Status* |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | S1 | Walking skeleton | relay, core, app | PL-01, PL-08, PL-15 | — | |
| M2 | S1 | Categories | core, app | PL-09, PL-21 | M1 (core.Transaction, PL-08 output) | |

## M1: Walking skeleton
**Outline:** One linked account syncs through the relay and its transactions list in the app.
```

When a stage gets more than one milestone, give each section a `**Skills:**` and a `**References:**` line from the stage's (`milestone_skills`, `milestone_references`); every stage skill and reference lands on at least one milestone (`stage_unrouted` empty). A single milestone inherits its stage's. Then `dt roadmap` (the stage → Split). The cut is presented with the first plan at the gate; until a milestone is Planned, its row may be re-cut, and a dropped row becomes a retired line whose ID is never reused.

## 3. Scope the milestone

From the milestone row and the pipelines: the spec IDs, packages, pipelines and exit criteria. A milestone row from before stages may still have Packages or Pipelines `—`; `dt` then infers them as `pipelines_inferred`: confirm them and write them into the row.

**Size is the critical path:** the longest `Depends on` chain, interface and system tasks included, at most about 15 (`dt status` `critical_path`; `dt lint` errors `milestone-size` above 15). Tasks that don't depend on each other don't add up, so independent tracks (relay beside core, meter readings beside weather) stay in one milestone, whatever the plan's total. Over the cap: mitigate an edge with an interface, fold (`phase0.md`), or re-cut the stage (step 2) along a dependency line. A single task belongs in an existing milestone.

Every spec ID the milestone's pipelines cover lands on a task or under `**Deferred:**` with a target and reason (`RES-07 (to M4: needs day-ahead forecasts)`), and so does every step of those pipelines that gets no task, by name (`PL-03.PPOAgent (to M3: fish need it first)`). Check each pipeline's step table row by row, rows a later stage's design added included (a library call such as `PL-07.daily_bars`). `uncovered` and `steps_unplanned` must end empty.

## S1: Snake + shared core
**Outline:** I can train my own from-scratch DQN on Snake in under an hour, see its learning curve beat random and scripted agents, switch off parts of the agent to see what breaks, and watch a replay, all on a shared core the next environment can reuse.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing

## M1: Train an agent
**Outline:** `python -m rllab train --env snake` trains the hand-written DQN, with any of the 3 switches off, on the shared core, and keeps its record and trained agent in `runs/<name>/`.
**Skills:** —
**References:** —
**Exit:** One command trains the full agent or any ablation on Snake or the corridor; TC-01–TC-03 pass and a 1,000-game Snake run finishes in ≤ 60 minutes (QR-01).
**Plan:** [../tasks/plan.md](../tasks/plan.md)

## M2: Baselines, curves and replay
**Outline:** Score the random and scripted baselines on the held-out games, draw saved runs' learning curves against them, and watch any saved agent or baseline play a seeded game.
**Skills:** —
**References:** —
**Exit:** `rllab baselines`, `rllab curves` and `rllab replay` run on M1's saved runs; TC-04, TC-05 and TC-12 pass.

## M3: The study
**Outline:** One unattended command trains the full agent and its 3 ablations × 5 seeds, scores both baselines, runs the comparisons and writes the report and chart; the notes check reports what the learner's page still lacks. Its exit holds the stage's bar: the full agent beats both baselines (TC-11).
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing
**References:** —
**Exit:** `rllab study --env snake` finishes unattended in ≤ 10 hours and the full agent beats both baselines; TC-06–TC-11 and TC-13–TC-15 pass.

## S2: Fish tank + policy gradients
**Outline:** I can train my own hand-written REINFORCE, actor-critic and PPO agents to steer a tank of fish away from a bouncing ball with continuous moves, see each rung beat random and scripted fish, switch off each added piece to see what breaks, and watch a replay, on the same shared core, with Snake still running unchanged.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing

## M4: Train the ladder on the tank
**Outline:** `python -m rllab train --env tank --agent pg --rung 1|2|3` trains each hand-written rung, with any of rung 3's switches off, on the fish tank (and the DQN on its compass twin), through the same core; the continuous line proves the core first, and Snake's results don't move.
**Skills:** —
**References:** —
**Exit:** One command trains any rung or rung-3 ablation on the tank or the line; the line is solved through the unchanged core (CORE-07), Snake's 200-game record matches M1's (CORE-09), and a 1,000-game rung-3 run with 5 fish finishes in ≤ 60 minutes (QR-01); S2's PL-01 golden cases pass.

## M5: Tank baselines, curves and replay
**Outline:** Score the random and scripted fish on the tank's held-out games, draw tank and compass-tank runs' learning curves against them, and watch any saved rung or fish baseline play a seeded game with the ball, wall and headings drawn.
**Skills:** —
**References:** —
**Exit:** `rllab baselines --env tank`, `rllab curves` and `rllab replay` run on M4's tank and line runs; the random fish's band (BASE-01) and the scripted fish's cases (BASE-04) pass, and a compass-tank DQN run is drawn against the tank's baselines (TWIN-02).

## M6: The tank study
**Outline:** One unattended command trains the 3 rungs and rung 3's 3 ablations × 5 seeds, scores both fish baselines, runs the baseline, ladder and ablation comparisons and writes the report and charts; the notes check covers the ladder's sections. Its exit holds the stage's bar: each rung beats random and scripted fish.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing
**References:** —
**Exit:** `rllab study --env tank` finishes unattended in ≤ 15 hours (QR-02); every rung's comparisons with both baselines and with the rung below are reported (EVAL-03, PG-10), and each rung beats both baselines; `rllab notes --env tank` checks the ladder's sections (EXPL-04).

## S3: Predator-prey + self-play
**Outline:** I can train a hand-written PPO predator and a shared prey policy against each other in the fish tank, where prey must eat pellets to survive, by taking turns, then learning at once, then self-play against past versions, all within about an hour, and see each side beat random and scripted opponents and later generations beat earlier ones in a cross-play table, on the same shared core, with Snake and the tank still running unchanged.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing

# rllab — Pipelines

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-01: Train an agent
**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipeline.py::run_train` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab train --env snake --seed 1 [--agent dqn|pg] [--rung 1|2|3] [--no-memory] [--no-frozen] [--no-decay] [--no-value] [--no-cap] [--no-rescale] [--fish 5] [--compass] [--prey 5] [--selfplay turns|simultaneous|selfplay] [--generations 10] [--games 1000] [--name NAME]`
**Purpose:** Train one hand-written learner (the DQN, or a rung of the policy-gradient ladder) on an environment, with any of its parts switched off, or train both sides of a two-sided environment against each other by taking turns, learning at once or self-play, and keep the record, each side's trained agent and its generations in `runs/<env>/<name>/`.

5	## PL-01: Train an agent
6	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipeline.py::run_train` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab train --env snake --seed 1 [--agent dqn|pg] [--rung 1|2|3] [--no-memory] [--no-frozen] [--no-decay] [--no-value] [--no-cap] [--no-rescale] [--fish 5] [--compass] [--prey 5] [--selfplay turns|simultaneous|selfplay] [--generations 10] [--games 1000] [--name NAME]`
7	**Purpose:** Train one hand-written learner (the DQN, or a rung of the policy-gradient ladder) on an environment, with any of its parts switched off, or train both sides of a two-sided environment against each other by taking turns, learning at once or self-play, and keep the record, each side's trained agent and its generations in `runs/<env>/<name>/`.
8	**Covers:** CORE-01–CORE-10, SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, TANK-01–TANK-10, HUNT-01–HUNT-12, TWIN-01, TWIN-02, AGENT-01–AGENT-06, PG-01–PG-09, PG-12, SELF-01–SELF-05, SELF-07, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08, EVAL-01, EVAL-02, LOG-01–LOG-05, QR-01, QR-03–QR-05

10	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
11	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
12	| 1 | `train_config` | cli.args | flags → RunConfig | `agents` keyed by the environment's side names from a plain match (`snake`; `walker`; `fish`; `point`; `predator` and `prey`; `chaser` and `runner`): on a one-sided environment `--agent dqn` switches into AgentConfig, `--agent pg` rung and switches into PGConfig; on the hunt both sides `AgentSpec("pg", PGConfig(rung=3))`, `--agent`, `--rung` or a switch refused naming it; on the chase both sides `pg` at `--rung`; a Snake switch with `pg` or a tank switch with `dqn` refused, naming the switch and the agent; `--fish`/`--compass`/`--prey` into `env_options`; on a two-sided environment `selfplay` from `--selfplay` (default `selfplay` on the hunt, `simultaneous` on the chase; `first` `prey` on the hunt, `chaser` on the chase) and `generations` from `--generations` (default 10, else 0); cadence from `eval_schedule`; default name `<variant>-s<seed>`, the variant a self-play rung's name on a two-sided environment | LOG-01, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08, SELF-01, SELF-02 |
13	| 2 | `eval_schedule` | registry.schedule | env name, n games → EvalSchedule | plain match: `(50, 100)` for snake and corridor, `(max(1, n // 10), 20)` for tank, hunt, line and chase (AD-15) | EVAL-02 |
14	| 3 | `make_run` | registry.run | RunConfig → RunSetup | `make_env` with the config's `env_options`; `make_agent` per side whose `AgentSpec` is a learner; opponents for a two-sided environment by `load_agent(<"scripted", or "random" where the side has none>, env, side)` per side, so the chase gets a `RandomAgent` built for each side (AD-25); with `config.selfplay`, `make_schedule` with a loader taking `load_agent`'s generation rules (AD-20, AD-22) | CORE-10, SELF-01, EVAL-02 |
15	| 4 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | plain match on `snake`, `corridor`, `tank`, `hunt`, `line` or `chase`, passing the tank's `fish` and `compass` and the hunt's `prey` | CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10 |
16	| 5 | `SnakeEnv` | snake.env | size → Env | 20 × 20 rules, 11 facts, rewards, step limit, frame; one side `snake`, id 0 | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, CORE-01–CORE-04 |
17	| 6 | `CorridorEnv` | corridor.env | length, max steps → Env | one-hot position, +1 at the right end; one side `walker`, id 0 | CORE-05, CORE-06 |
18	| 7 | `TankEnv` | tank.env | fish, compass → Env | NumPy rules in tank units (AD-14): the ball's mirror bounce, removal under 1.3, the 1,000-step limit, +1 per step survived; turning, swimming, rays and start spots by `arena`; the 25 numbers per living fish; frame with wall, ball and heading segments; `compass` swaps in `Choices(8)` at speed 0.3; one side `fish` | TANK-01–TANK-10, TWIN-01, CORE-02–CORE-04 |
19	| 8 | `HuntEnv` | hunt.env | prey → Env | NumPy rules in HUNT's order (AD-21): swim by `arena.swim` (limits 9.5 and 9.7), catch under 0.8, eat within 0.5 (closer prey, then lower id) with the respawn by `arena.random_spots` at least 0.5 from living prey, energy in thousandths (−2, +300, cap 1,000) and starving at 0; 10 pellets; the 1,000-step limit; rewards and scores per HUNT-10 and HUNT-11; observations from `arena`'s rays (34 per prey, 17 for the predator); frame with wall, pellets, headings and energy bars; sides `predator` (id 0) and `prey` (ids 1–n) | HUNT-01–HUNT-12, CORE-02–CORE-04 |
20	| 9 | `swim` | arena.motion | positions, headings, turns, speeds, limit → Placement | turn, then move along the new heading, the centre stopped at `limit` along that heading, all fish at once | TANK-02, TANK-08, HUNT-02, HUNT-03 |
21	| 10 | `ray_wall` | arena.sight | positions, headings → Readings | 8 rays at heading + 45°·k, distance to the wall circle / 4, capped at 1 | TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09 |
22	| 11 | `ray_circles` | arena.sight | positions, headings, centres, radii → Readings | distance along each ray to the nearest circle's edge / 4, 1 when none within reach, 0 from inside, each fish skipping itself | TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09 |
23	| 12 | `random_spots` | arena.spots | generator, n, max radius, away, min distance → Spots | uniform over the disc, redrawn until `min_dist` from every `away` point, at most 10,000 draws | TANK-03, HUNT-01, HUNT-06 |
24	| 13 | `LineEnv` | line.env | points, length, max steps → Env | each point moves by its push, held at 0; +1 and removed at `length`; ends when all are removed or at `max_steps`; one side `point` | CORE-07, CORE-08 |
25	| 14 | `ChaseEnv` | chase.env | length, max steps → Env | chaser at 0, runner at `length`, both pushing within `[0, length]`; a touch within 0.5 gives the chaser +1 and ends the game, else the runner +1; ends at `max_steps`; sides `chaser` (id 0) and `runner` (id 1) | CORE-10 |
26	| 15 | `make_agent` | registry.learners | RunConfig, Env, side → LearningAgent | plain match on the side's `AgentSpec.kind`: `dqn` → `DQNAgent`, `pg` → `PGAgent` on that side's size and moves, then `check_agent` for that side | CORE-07, SELF-01, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08 |
27	| 16 | `DQNAgent` | agent.dqn | obs size, choice count, AgentConfig, seed → LearningAgent | 11 → 256 → 3 MLP learning in `learn` from the latest Transition only, ε fixed at 0.1, targets from the current net (every switch off); `end_game` returns `None`; `torch.save` state dict | AGENT-01–AGENT-06, ABL-01–ABL-03, TWIN-02, QR-03, QR-04 |
28	| 17 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | obs size, Controls, PGConfig, seed → LearningAgent | rung 1 for every rung: obs → 64 → 64 → centres MLP with a learned log width, normal draws clipped to the limits, centre when greedy; `learn` stores the step; `end_game` weights each step's log-probability by the discounted return (0.99) to that fish's end and makes one Adam step; `torch.save` state dict; `load` checks the side and seeds its draws (AD-13, AD-22) | PG-01–PG-06, PG-12, SELF-01, SELF-07, QR-03 |
29	| 18 | `make_schedule` | selfplay.schedule | SelfPlayConfig, learners, n games, seed, loader → Schedule | plain match on `config.rung`; refuses anything but 2 sides, a `first` that isn't one, or `blocks` not dividing `n_games` | SELF-03–SELF-05 |
30	| 19 | `TurnsSchedule` | selfplay.turns | SelfPlayConfig, learners, n games → Schedule | game g in block `g · blocks // n_games`, block 0 learning `first`, sides alternating, the other side's current learner playing frozen; `add_generation` ignored | SELF-03, QR-03 |
31	| 20 | `SimultaneousSchedule` | selfplay.simultaneous | learners → Schedule | every game both current learners, both learning; `add_generation` ignored | SELF-04, QR-03 |
32	| 21 | `PoolSchedule` | selfplay.pool | SelfPlayConfig, learners, seed, loader → Schedule | current against current while the opponent has no generation; else a kind drawn with shares 0.5, 0.25, 0.25 and a past generation uniformly from those added, from a generator built from the run seed; each past generation loaded once by the loader and cached, playing frozen (AD-22) | SELF-05, QR-03, QR-05 |
33	| 22 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | `random` → `RandomAgent(side)` (AD-25); `scripted` → `ScriptedAgent` on Snake, `ScriptedFish` on the continuous tank, `ScriptedPredator` or `ScriptedPrey` on the hunt's sides, refused elsewhere naming the environment and side; a run folder via its record's `AgentSpec` and `DQNAgent.load` or `PGAgent.load` at `agent_path`, `<run>@<n>` its generation n | SELF-07, REPLAY-01 |
34	| 23 | `RandomAgent` | baselines.uniform | Side → Agent | from that side's moves only (AD-25): uniform over `range(n)` for Choices, each control uniform within its limits for Controls, `kind` following them; generator reseeded in `reset` | BASE-01 |
35	| 24 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.predator | name → Agent | from the rays only: speed 0.35; a prey in reach → turn 10° toward the ray seeing it closest (ray 0 straight, ray 4 left); else the wall within 1 unit ahead → 10° toward more room, left on a tie; else straight | BASE-05 |
36	| 25 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.prey | name → Agent | from the rays only: speed 0.3; the wall within 1 unit ahead → 15° toward more room; else away from the ray seeing the predator closest; else toward the ray seeing a pellet closest; else straight | BASE-06 |
37	| 26 | `train_run` | train.loop | Env, LearningAgent, RunConfig, Lineup, Schedule → RunRecord | game loop: each game `schedule.match(game)`, or every learner learning with no schedule; `begin_game` for learning sides; `act(explore=True)` per living id in id order through its side's agent, `step`, `learn` per learning side's Transition in id order, `end_game` for learning sides (AD-12, AD-20); every `eval_every` games `evaluate` each learning side on the first `eval_games` held-out seeds against `opponents` for the other sides, plus each side's mean loss; every `n_games / generations` games `save_generation` per learning side and `add_generation`; the final check over all 100 per side; each learner saved; failed record on any error, generations kept | CORE-05, CORE-07–CORE-10, AGENT-02, EVAL-02, LOG-02, LOG-03, SELF-02, QR-01, QR-05 |
38	| 27 | `check_env` | core.check | Env → Env | `hasattr` on the 5 behaviours; sides non-empty with distinct names and disjoint ids | CORE-05 |
39	| 28 | `check_agent` | core.check | Agent, Env → Lineup | a lone agent on a one-sided environment becomes `{side: agent}`; each side's agent's `kind` against its side's moves; a side with no agent raises `MissingSideError` | CORE-07, CORE-10 |
40	| 29 | `training_seeds` | evaluate.seeds | run seed, n games → Seeds | generator from the run seed over `[0, 1_000_000)` (AD-04) | EVAL-01, QR-05 |
41	| 30 | `create_run` | runs.store | RunConfig → RunWriter | new `root/<env>/<name>/` folder, config written at once; refuses an existing name (AD-15) | LOG-01 |
42	| 31 | `JsonRunWriter` | runs.writer | RunConfig, path → RunWriter | record in memory, atomic rewrite on `add_eval`, `save_generation` and `finish` (with `final` per side); `save_agent` and `save_generation` write `torch.save` files at `agent_path` (AD-05) | LOG-02–LOG-05, SELF-02 |
43	| 32 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | `run/<side>.pt`, or `run/generations/<side>-<NN>.pt` | LOG-04, SELF-02 |
44	| 33 | `evaluate` | evaluate.score | Env, Lineup, seeds, side → EvalResult | `check_agent`, then `play_game` greedily on each seed; `side`'s scores, mean and std | EVAL-02, AGENT-03, PG-02 |
45	| 34 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Lineup, seed → GameResult | reset the environment and each distinct agent; each step ask every living id, in id order, its side's agent, stop asking a removed one, until done; every side's score | CORE-02, CORE-08, AGENT-03 |

47	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
48	```python
49	def run_train(args: Namespace) -> RunRecord:
50	    config = train_config(args)
51	    setup = make_run(config)
52	    return train_run(setup.env, setup.learners, config, opponents=setup.opponents, schedule=setup.schedule, root=args.root)
53	```

55	## PL-02: Score the baselines
56	**Entry:** `src/rllab/study/pipelines/pl02_baselines.py::run_baselines` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab baselines --env snake|tank|hunt`
57	**Purpose:** Score the random and scripted agents of an environment, for each side, on the same 100 held-out games as its learners, so their curves have bars to clear.
58	**Covers:** BASE-01–BASE-06, EVAL-01, REPLAY-01
59	
60	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
61	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
62	| 1 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built (the tank with its default 5 fish and the hunt with its default 5 prey, the only baselines their charts show) | CORE-06 |
63	| 2 | `score_baselines` | study.baselines | Env, root → RunRecord (one per baseline) | one-sided: `baseline-random` and `baseline-scripted`; two-sided: `baseline-random-<side>` per side (that side random, every other scripted) and `baseline-scripted` (every side scripted) (AD-24); per baseline an existing finished record is read back with `load_record`; otherwise create it under the environment's folder, `evaluate` each side on `EVAL_SEEDS`, `add_eval`, `finish` with those results as `final` | BASE-03, EVAL-01 |
64	| 3 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
65	| 4 | `RandomAgent` | baselines.uniform | Side → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-01 |
66	| 5 | `ScriptedAgent` | baselines.scripted | name → Agent | toward the food along the larger gap; non-fatal fallback preferring straight | BASE-02 |
67	| 6 | `ScriptedFish` | baselines.fish | name → Agent | from the rays only: speed 0.3; the wall within 1 unit ahead → turn 15° toward more room; else away from the ray seeing the ball closest; else straight | BASE-04 |
68	| 7 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.predator | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-05 |
69	| 8 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.prey | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-06 |
70	| 9 | `create_run` | runs.store | RunConfig → RunWriter | PL-01 as built | LOG-01 |
71	| 10 | `evaluate` | evaluate.score | Env, Lineup, seeds, side → EvalResult | PL-01 as built | BASE-03 |
72	| 11 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Lineup, seed → GameResult | PL-01 as built | BASE-03 |
73	| 12 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | read `record.json` | LOG-01 |
74	
75	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
76	```python
77	def run_baselines(args: Namespace) -> tuple[RunRecord, ...]:
78	    env = make_env(args.env)
79	    return score_baselines(env, root=args.root)
80	```

82	## PL-03: Draw learning curves
83	**Entry:** `src/rllab/curves/pipeline.py::run_curves` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab curves runs/tank/ppo-s1 runs/tank/no-cap-s1 [--side prey] --out chart.png`
84	**Purpose:** Redraw learning curves from saved run records, one side per chart, with that side's baselines as flat lines, without training again.
85	**Covers:** CURVE-01–CURVE-07, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, TWIN-02, QR-04
86	
87	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
88	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
89	| 1 | `load_records` | curves.inputs | run paths → RunRecord (one per path) | `load_record` per path; skips and names a record with no evaluation points | CURVE-05 |
90	| 2 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | CURVE-05 |
91	| 3 | `find_baselines` | curves.inputs | root, RunRecord → RunRecord (each baseline found) | `root/<env>/baseline-*` records of the records' environment, so compass-tank runs get the tank's and runs of any fish or prey count get the default bars | CURVE-03, TWIN-02 |
92	| 4 | `plot_curves` | curves.plot | RunRecord, side → CurvePlot | Matplotlib Agg PNG of `side`'s evaluation results (the only side when none is given): one mean line with a min–max band per config minus seed, labelled `full` or the DQN's switches off, a rung's name plus any switch off, a self-play rung's name, `compass` added; each baseline whose other sides play `scripted` as a flat line (AD-07) | CURVE-01–CURVE-07, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, QR-04 |
93	
94	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
95	```python
96	def run_curves(args: Namespace) -> CurvePlot:
97	    records = load_records(args.runs)
98	    baselines = find_baselines(args.root, records)
99	    return plot_curves(records, args.out, baselines=baselines, side=args.side)
100	```

102	## PL-04: Run the study
103	**Entry:** `src/rllab/study/pipelines/pl04_study.py::run_study` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab study --env snake|tank|hunt [--skip turns]`
104	**Purpose:** Run an environment's whole study unattended (every learner and ablation × 5 seeds, both baselines, and in the predator-prey tank every run's cross-play table) and get the report, charts and tables for its notes page.
105	**Covers:** ABL-01–ABL-08, PG-10, PG-11, EVAL-03, EVAL-04, CURVE-06, CURVE-07, CROSS-02–CROSS-04, SELF-06, SELF-08, EXPL-02–EXPL-05, QR-02
106	
107	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
108	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
109	| 1 | `study_plan` | study.plan | env name → StudyPlan | `STUDIES[env]` (AD-16, AD-24); `KeyError` naming the studied environments | ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08 |
110	| 2 | `study_configs` | study.plan | StudyPlan, seeds, n games, skip → RunConfig (one per variant and seed) | every variant but the skipped ones × seed, named `<variant>-s<seed>`, its `Variant`'s `agents`, `selfplay` and `generations`, the plan's `env_options` and `eval_schedule`'s cadence; `ValueError` naming a skipped variant that isn't optional | ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08, SELF-06 |
111	| 3 | `eval_schedule` | registry.schedule | env name, n games → EvalSchedule | PL-01 as built | EVAL-02 |
112	| 4 | `train_variants` | study.runner | RunConfig, root, workers → RunRecord (one per config) | `ProcessPoolExecutor`, one thread per run; each worker: `make_run`, `train_run`; a failed run's record read back (AD-03); no resume: a run whose folder exists is refused by `create_run` and comes back missing, so re-running means deleting its folder | QR-02 |
113	| 5 | `make_run` | registry.run | RunConfig → RunSetup | PL-01 as built | SELF-01 |
114	| 6 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-06 |

116	| 8 | `DQNAgent` | agent.dqn | obs size, choice count, AgentConfig, seed → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built | ABL-01–ABL-03 |
117	| 9 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | obs size, Controls, PGConfig, seed → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built | ABL-06–ABL-08, SELF-01 |
118	| 10 | `make_schedule` | selfplay.schedule | SelfPlayConfig, learners, n games, seed, loader → Schedule | PL-01 as built | SELF-03–SELF-05 |
119	| 11 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | SELF-07 |
120	| 12 | `train_run` | train.loop | Env, LearningAgent, RunConfig, Lineup, Schedule → RunRecord | PL-01 as built | QR-02 |
121	| 13 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | LOG-05 |
122	| 14 | `play_tables` | study.crossplay | StudyPlan, RunRecord, root, workers → CrossTable (one per run with a generation per side) | none when the plan has no cross-play; else per run, finished or not, that saved at least one generation per side, in the same pool: `load_table` when its `crossplay.json` exists, else `build_table` over the saved generations, so an unfinished run's table comes back not `complete` (AD-23, CROSS edge case) | CROSS-02, QR-02 |
123	| 15 | `build_table` | crossplay.build | run folder → CrossTable | PL-07 as built | CROSS-01 |
124	| 16 | `load_table` | crossplay.store | path → CrossTable | PL-07 as built | CROSS-02 |
125	| 17 | `score_baselines` | study.baselines | Env, root → RunRecord (one per baseline) | PL-02 as built | BASE-03 |
126	| 18 | `compare_runs` | study.compare | StudyPlan, RunRecord, baselines, CrossTable → Comparison (per family) | `final` scores into `compare_scores` and tables into `compare_generations`, each family corrected within itself (AD-16): every learner and side against each baseline, a two-sided side's bars being the baselines whose other sides play `scripted`; each rung against the one below in a ladder; each ablation against `full`; each side's last generation against its first over complete tables (AD-23); a skipped optional learner leaves every family; unfinished runs left out or `missing` as AD-09 says | ABL-04, PG-10, EVAL-03, CROSS-03, CROSS-04, SELF-06 |
127	| 19 | `compare_scores` | study.stats | score arrays, label, n comparisons → Comparison | one-sided test paired by held-out game on per-game seed means, Bonferroni over `n_comparisons`, built with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill (AD-09) | ABL-04, PG-10, EVAL-03 |
128	| 20 | `compare_generations` | study.generations | score arrays, label, n comparisons → Comparison | one-sided test of the last generation against the first, paired by run, opponent generation and game, Bonferroni over `n_comparisons`, built with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill (AD-23) | CROSS-03 |
129	| 21 | `draw_charts` | study.charts | StudyPlan, RunRecord, baselines, out folder → CurvePlot (one per chart) | one-sided: `curves.png` of `full`, its ablations and the baselines, and `ladder.png` of the learners and the baselines in a ladder; two-sided: `curves-<side>.png` per side of the learners and that side's baselines | EXPL-03, CURVE-06, CURVE-07 |
130	| 22 | `plot_curves` | curves.plot | RunRecord, side → CurvePlot | PL-03 as built | EXPL-03, CURVE-07 |
131	| 23 | `write_tables` | study.tables | StudyPlan, CrossTable, out folder → TablePage (one per rung) | none without cross-play; else `crossplay-<rung>.md` per rung with tables, by `write_table` over that rung's runs, each incomplete table's run listed under it as incomplete | CROSS-02, EXPL-05 |
132	| 24 | `write_table` | crossplay.markdown | CrossTable, out, title → TablePage | PL-07 as built | CROSS-02 |
133	| 25 | `write_report` | study.report | StudyPlan, Comparison, CurvePlot, TablePage, RunRecord → StudyReport | `root/<env>/study/report.md`: each family's comparisons with their test results, each learner's (and side's) spread across seeds, incomplete runs and skipped rungs, the ABL-05, PG-11 and SELF-08 finding lines, links to the charts and tables, a section per ablation, per rung above the first in a ladder and per self-play rung | ABL-05, PG-10, PG-11, EVAL-04, SELF-08, EXPL-02, EXPL-04, EXPL-05 |
134	
135	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
136	```python
137	def run_study(env_name="snake", seeds=(1, 2, 3, 4, 5), *, n_games=1_000, workers=None, skip=(), root=Path("runs")) -> StudyReport:
138	    plan = study_plan(env_name)
139	    configs = study_configs(plan, seeds, n_games, skip=skip)
140	    records = train_variants(configs, root, workers=workers)
141	    tables = play_tables(plan, records, root, workers=workers)
142	    env = make_env(plan.env, **dict(plan.env_options))
143	    baselines = score_baselines(env, root=root)
144	    comparisons = compare_runs(plan, records, baselines, tables)
145	    charts = draw_charts(plan, records, baselines, root / plan.env / "study")
146	    table_files = write_tables(plan, tables, root / plan.env / "study")
147	    return write_report(plan, comparisons, charts, table_files, records, root)
148	```

150	## PL-05: Watch a replay
151	**Entry:** `src/rllab/replay/pipeline.py::run_replay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab replay runs/tank/ppo-s1 --env tank --seed 1000000 [--fps 10]`, or one `<side>=<spec>` per side: `python -m rllab replay predator=runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@10 prey=scripted --env hunt --seed 1000000`
152	**Purpose:** Watch any saved agent, generation or baseline, one per side, play one seeded game frame by frame, with pause, step and speed controls.
153	**Covers:** REPLAY-01–REPLAY-05, CORE-04, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, AGENT-06, PG-12, SELF-07, QR-04
154	
155	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
156	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
157	| 1 | `replay_specs` | replay.specs | spec words → AgentSpecs | a lone word stays one spec; each `<side>=<spec>` word becomes one side's entry; a side named twice refused naming it | REPLAY-01 |
158	| 2 | `record_replay` | replay.record | AgentSpecs, env name, seed → Replay | `make_env` with the first run folder's own `env_options` (defaults for baselines only), `load_agent` per side, then `play_game` greedily with frames; a side with no spec raises `MissingSideError` before any window opens | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-04, REPLAY-05, CORE-10 |
159	| 3 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
160	| 4 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-06 |
161	| 5 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01, SELF-07 |
162	| 6 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
163	| 7 | `RandomAgent` | baselines.uniform | Side → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
164	| 8 | `ScriptedAgent` | baselines.scripted | name → Agent | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
165	| 9 | `ScriptedFish` | baselines.fish | name → Agent | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
166	| 10 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.predator | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
167	| 11 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.prey | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
168	| 12 | `DQNAgent` | agent.dqn | path, Side → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built (`DQNAgent.load`) | AGENT-06 |

172	
173	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
174	```python
175	def run_replay(args: Namespace) -> Playback:
176	    specs = replay_specs(args.agents)
177	    replay = record_replay(specs, args.env, args.seed)
178	    return show(replay, fps=args.fps)
179	```

197	## PL-07: Build a cross-play table
198	**Entry:** `src/rllab/crossplay/pipeline.py::run_crossplay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab crossplay runs/hunt/selfplay-s1 runs/hunt/selfplay-s2 --out crossplay-selfplay.md [--title selfplay]`
199	**Purpose:** Play every saved generation of one side against every saved generation of the other on the same 20 held-out games, from the saved files only, and get the table, averaged over the runs given, as Markdown.
200	**Covers:** CROSS-01, CROSS-02, SELF-07, QR-04
201	
202	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
203	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
204	| 1 | `gather_tables` | crossplay.tables | run paths → CrossTable (one per run) | per run, `load_table` when its `crossplay.json` exists, else `build_table`; a run with no generations refused, naming it | CROSS-02 |
205	| 2 | `load_table` | crossplay.store | path → CrossTable | read `crossplay.json` | CROSS-02 |
206	| 3 | `build_table` | crossplay.build | run folder → CrossTable | `load_record`, `make_env` with its `env_options`, each saved generation by `load_agent("<run>@<n>", env, side)`, every predator generation against every prey generation with `play_game(explore=False)` on the first 20 `EVAL_SEEDS`; both sides' per-game scores written to `run/crossplay.json`; never trains (AD-23) | CROSS-01, CROSS-02 |
207	| 4 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | CROSS-01 |
208	| 5 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built | CROSS-01 |
209	| 6 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | SELF-07 |
210	| 7 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | PL-01 as built | SELF-07 |
211	| 8 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | path, Side → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built (`PGAgent.load`) | SELF-07 |
212	| 9 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Lineup, seed → GameResult | PL-01 as built | CROSS-01 |
213	| 10 | `write_table` | crossplay.markdown | CrossTable, out, title → TablePage | one Markdown table: a row per predator generation, a column per prey generation, each cell `predator mean / prey mean` over every table's games; incomplete runs listed under it and in the result | CROSS-02, QR-04 |
214	
215	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
216	```python
217	def run_crossplay(args: Namespace) -> TablePage:
218	    tables = gather_tables(args.runs)
219	    return write_table(tables, args.out, title=args.title)
220	```

314	## Risks
315	| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |
316	|---|---|---|
317	| A 1,000-game Snake run takes over 60 minutes on the laptop CPU | QR-01 fails, and M3's study passes 10 hours | M1-T19's timing test measures it before M3; AD-03's one thread per run |
318	| The DQN doesn't solve the corridor with the tutorial settings | TC-01 fails | M1-T15–M1-T17 test each part alone first, so a failure points at one file; tune AD-02's settings before questioning the design |
319	| Non-determinism in torch on CPU | TC-02 fails, and CORE-09's before/after check in M4 has no stable "before" | AD-04: per-object generators, one thread, deterministic algorithms; M1-T04 and M1-T09 pin seeded behavior early |
320	| The several-agent loop is only exercised on a hand-built line double in M1 | A removal bug surfaces only when M4 brings the real line and tank | M1-T08 and M1-T13 test removal on the 2-point line double; M4's system task runs CORE-07 and CORE-08 on the real `LineEnv` |
321	
322	## Open questions
323	- `boundaries_unfrozen` lists the tank, line, pg, baselines, `registry.load_agent`, curves, replay and study symbols, and the tank/line cases of core types: none is on an M1 step, so M2–M6's interface tasks freeze them (M4 adds the seeded tank and line cases to the core contract folders).
324	- M1 builds only the Snake and corridor branches of `make_env`, `train_config` and `make_agent` (the `dqn` agent); their tank, line and `pg` branches are thicken tasks in M4.

1	# rllab — Contract
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

5	## core
6	
7	### core.Choices: type
8	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Choices: n: int`
9	- **Invariants:** `n` ≥ 1; a move is an `int` in `range(n)` (CORE-03)
10	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Choices/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 moves; the compass tank's 8 moves
11	- **Used by:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate, registry
12	
13	### core.Controls: type
14	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Controls: names: tuple[str, ...]; low: tuple[float, ...]; high: tuple[float, ...]`
15	- **Invariants:** the three tuples have the same length ≥ 1; `low[i] < high[i]`; a move is a float64 array of length `len(names)` with `low ≤ move ≤ high` element-wise (CORE-03)
16	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Controls/ · **Cases:** the tank's and the prey's `("turn", "speed")`, −15 to 15 degrees and 0 to 0.3; the predator's −10 to 10 degrees and 0 to 0.35; the line's `("push",)`, −1 to 1
17	- **Used by:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate, registry
18	
19	### core.Moves: type
20	`Moves: TypeAlias = Choices | Controls`
21	- **Invariants:** every side reports exactly one (CORE-03)
22	- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Moves/ · **Cases:** `Choices(3)`; the tank's `Controls`
23	- **Used by:** core.Side, core.InvalidMoveError, registry
24	
25	### core.Move: type
26	`Move: TypeAlias = int | np.ndarray`
27	- **Invariants:** an `int` for `Choices`; a float64 array shaped `(len(names),)` for `Controls`
28	- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Move/ · **Cases:** Snake move 2; tank move `[15.0, 0.3]`; predator move `[-10.0, 0.35]`
29	- **Used by:** core.Env, core.Agent, core.Transition, evaluate, train
30	
31	### core.Side: type
32	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Side: name: str; obs_size: int; moves: Moves; ids: tuple[int, ...]`
33	- **Invariants:** `name` is a lowercase word, unique among the environment's sides; `ids` is non-empty, ascending and disjoint from every other side's; every observation of an id in `ids` has length `obs_size`, and its moves follow `moves` (CORE-03, AD-19)
34	- **Boundary:** every environment, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Side/ · **Cases:** Snake's `Side("snake", 11, Choices(3), (0,))`; the 5-fish tank's `fish` side, ids 0–4; the hunt's `predator` side (17, the predator's controls, id 0) and `prey` side (34, the tank's controls, ids 1–5)
35	- **Used by:** core.Env, agent, pg, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay
36	
37	### core.Scores: type
38	`Scores: TypeAlias = dict[str, float]`
39	- **Invariants:** one entry per side name: Snake food eaten; the tank's fish mean steps survived; the hunt's predator prey caught and prey mean steps survived (HUNT-10, HUNT-11); the line's points that reached the end; the corridor 1 if it reached the end; the chase's chaser 1 if it touched, runner steps untouched
40	- **Boundary:** every environment, evaluate, runs, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Scores/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": 4.0}`; `{"predator": 3.0, "prey": 550.0}`
41	- **Used by:** core.Step, core.Frame, evaluate, runs, replay
42	
43	### core.Env: type
44	```python
45	class Env(Protocol):
46	    name: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]
47	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
48	    def step(self, moves: Mapping[int, Move]) -> Step: ...
49	    def render(self) -> Frame: ...
50	```
51	- **Invariants:** `reset` returns one float32 observation per id of every side, of its side's `obs_size`; `step` takes one move per living id (CORE-02); the same seed and moves give identical steps and frames (CORE-01, CORE-02); Snake, the corridor, the tank and the line have one side, the hunt and the chase two (AD-19)
52	- **Errors:** a move outside its side's `moves` raises `InvalidMoveError`; a missing move for a living id, or a move for one that isn't, raises `WrongAgentsError`; either way the game doesn't change; `step` after the game ends raises `GameOverError`
53	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 6-step corridor game; a replay double of a recorded Snake game on seed 7 ending in a wall hit; a replay double of a recorded 2-point line game where one point is removed first; a replay double of a recorded 2-prey hunt game on seed 7 where the predator catches one prey
54	- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, baselines, agent, pg, crossplay
55	
56	### core.Step: type
57	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: dict[int, np.ndarray]; rewards: dict[int, float]; alive: frozenset[int]; done: bool; scores: Scores`
58	- **Invariants:** `obs` and `rewards` hold one entry for each id that was sent a move; `alive` ⊆ those ids, the ids asked for moves next (CORE-08); `done` exactly when `alive` is empty, including at a time limit, and the end of a game empties it (the hunt's predator is alive until then); `scores` is every side's score so far
59	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a Snake step that eats food (+10); a game-ending Snake step (−10, done); a corridor step that reaches the right end (+1, done); a tank step where 1 of 3 fish is removed; a line step where the first point reaches 5; a hunt step where the predator catches 2 prey (+2) and a prey eats a pellet
60	- **Used by:** evaluate, train
61	
62	### core.Circle: type
63	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Circle: x: float; y: float; r: float; color: str; fill: bool`
64	- **Invariants:** in the frame's cell units, origin at the top-left, y down; `color` is `#rrggbb`
65	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Circle/ · **Cases:** the tank wall (radius 10 at (10, 10), not filled); the ball; a pellet
66	- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay
67	
68	### core.Segment: type
69	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Segment: x1: float; y1: float; x2: float; y2: float; color: str`
70	- **Invariants:** as `Circle`'s units and colour
71	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Segment/ · **Cases:** a fish's heading line; a prey's energy bar
72	- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay
73	
74	### core.Frame: type
75	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: cells: np.ndarray; colors: dict[int, str]; step: int; scores: Scores; shapes: tuple[Circle | Segment, ...] = (); caption: str = ""`
76	- **Invariants:** `cells` is H×W uint8 cell codes and sets the drawing area; every code has a `#rrggbb` colour in `colors`; `shapes` are drawn over the cells in order; `caption` is extra text shown with the step and scores (CORE-04, AD-06); Snake uses codes 0 empty, 1 body, 2 head, 3 food and no shapes; the tank uses a blank 20 × 20 grid, its wall, ball and each living fish with a heading segment, and the caption `fish left: N`; the hunt the same grid and wall, its 10 pellets, the predator with a heading segment and each living prey with a heading segment and an energy bar of length 0.6 × energy, and the caption `prey left: N`
77	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** the first frame of Snake seed 7; a 1 × 5 corridor frame; the first frame of tank seed 2 with 5 fish; the first frame of hunt seed 2 with 5 prey
78	- **Used by:** replay, evaluate
79	
80	### core.Transition: type
81	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; action: Move; reward: float; next_obs: np.ndarray; done: bool; agent: int = 0`
82	- **Invariants:** one id's step: `obs` and `next_obs` have the same length; `done` is true on the step that id is removed or the game ends; `agent` is the id it acted as
83	- **Boundary:** train, agent, pg · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a non-final Snake step with reward 0; a final Snake step with reward −10; a tank fish's removal step (reward 0, done, agent 2); a predator's catch step (reward +1, agent 0)
84	- **Used by:** agent, pg, train
85	
86	### core.Agent: type
87	```python
88	class Agent(Protocol):
89	    name: str; kind: Literal["choices", "controls", "any"]
90	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> None: ...
91	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, env: Env, *, explore: bool) -> Move: ...
92	```
93	- **Invariants:** `kind` says which `Moves` it plays; `reset` is called once at the start of every game with that game's seed; `act` is called once per living id of the side it plays, in id order, on that id's observation, so one object plays every fish of a side with one policy (PG-04); with `explore=False`, the same seed and observations give the same moves (AGENT-03, PG-02, REPLAY-04)
94	- **Boundary:** agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, selfplay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded random-agent game on corridor seed 3; a replay double of a recorded random-agent tank game on seed 2 with 2 fish
95	- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, selfplay
96	
97	### core.Lineup: type
98	`Lineup: TypeAlias = Mapping[str, Agent]`
99	- **Invariants:** one agent per side name of the environment it plays; one agent may be named for more than one side (AD-19)
100	- **Boundary:** evaluate, train, registry, selfplay, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Lineup/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": the scripted agent}`; `{"predator": a rung-3 agent, "prey": the scripted prey}`
101	- **Used by:** core.Match, core.check_agent, evaluate, train, registry
102	
103	### core.LearningAgent: type
104	```python
105	class LearningAgent(Agent, Protocol):
106	    def begin_game(self, game: int, n_games: int) -> None: ...
107	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> float | None: ...
108	    def end_game(self) -> float | None: ...
109	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
110	```
111	- **Invariants:** called only for games in which its side learns: `begin_game` sets up training game `game` of `n_games`, `learn` is called for each of its side's ids' steps and `end_game` once after the game; each returns the loss of the update it made, or `None` when it made none (AD-12, AD-20); in a game where its side is frozen it only acts
112	- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.LearningAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game corridor DQN run, with losses from `learn`; a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of rung 1, with losses from `end_game`
113	- **Used by:** train, registry, selfplay
114	
115	### core.Match: type
116	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Match: agents: Lineup; learning: frozenset[str]; label: str = ""`
117	- **Invariants:** `agents` names one agent per side; each side in `learning` plays the run's own learner for that side, and only those learners are told about the game; every other side plays frozen (CORE-10, AD-20); `label` names the kind of game (`both`, `past-prey`, `past-predator`, `block-3`)
118	- **Boundary:** train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Match/ · **Cases:** a one-sided Snake game learning `{"snake"}`; the current predator against prey generation 3, learning `{"predator"}`; a taking-turns block-1 game learning `{"prey"}`
119	- **Used by:** core.Schedule, train, selfplay
120	
121	### core.Schedule: type
122	```python
123	class Schedule(Protocol):
124	    def match(self, game: int) -> Match: ...
125	    def add_generation(self, side: str, number: int, path: Path) -> None: ...
126	```
127	- **Invariants:** `match` is called once before each training game, in game order; `add_generation` after each generation is saved, with its file; the same run seed gives the same matches (QR-05, AD-22)
128	- **Boundary:** train, selfplay, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 20-game self-play schedule with generations added after games 10 and 20; a replay double of a recorded 20-game taking-turns schedule
129	- **Used by:** train, registry
130	
131	### core.InvalidMoveError: type
132	`class InvalidMoveError(ValueError): agent: int; side: str; move: Move; allowed: Moves; step: int | None`
133	- **Invariants:** the message names the id, its side, the move and the allowed choices or each control with its limits, and, when raised by `evaluate.play_game`, the step number
134	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.InvalidMoveError/ · **Cases:** Snake given move 3; the tank given a turn of 20; the predator given a turn of 12
135	- **Used by:** evaluate, train
136	
137	### core.WrongAgentsError: type
138	`class WrongAgentsError(ValueError): missing: tuple[int, ...]; extra: tuple[int, ...]`
139	- **Invariants:** `missing` names the living ids with no move, `extra` the ids sent a move that aren't living; the message names each fish (TANK and HUNT edge cases)
140	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WrongAgentsError/ · **Cases:** a move for removed fish 1; living fish 3 with no move; no move for the predator
141	- **Used by:** evaluate, train
142	
143	### core.GameOverError: type
144	`class GameOverError(ValueError): ...`
145	- **Invariants:** the message says the game is over
146	- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.GameOverError/ · **Cases:** a step after a Snake wall hit; a step after the tank's last fish is removed; a step after the hunt's last prey is removed
147	- **Used by:** evaluate

149	### core.EnvCheckError: type
150	`class EnvCheckError(TypeError): missing: tuple[str, ...]`
151	- **Invariants:** `missing` lists every absent behaviour by attribute name, and `sides` when the sides are empty or two share a name or an id
152	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EnvCheckError/ · **Cases:** an object missing `render` and `step`; two sides sharing id 0

155	### core.MoveKindError: type
156	`class MoveKindError(TypeError): agent: str; agent_kind: str; side: str; env: str; env_kind: str`
157	- **Invariants:** the message names the agent and its kind, and the environment, the side and its kind (CORE edge case)
158	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MoveKindError/ · **Cases:** the DQN given the continuous tank; a rung given Snake
159	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

161	### core.MissingSideError: type
162	`class MissingSideError(ValueError): env: str; sides: tuple[str, ...]`
163	- **Invariants:** `sides` names every side with no agent; the message names them and the environment (CORE edge case)
164	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MissingSideError/ · **Cases:** the hunt given only a predator; a lone agent given to the chase
165	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

167	### core.NonFiniteLossError: type
168	`class NonFiniteLossError(FloatingPointError): update: int`
169	- **Invariants:** `update` is the agent's update count; `train.train_run` adds the game and step to the failed record's `error`
170	- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.NonFiniteLossError/ · **Cases:** a NaN loss on DQN update 12; an infinite loss on a rung's update 3
171	- **Used by:** agent, pg, train

173	### core.check_env: Start-up environment check
174	`def check_env(env: object) -> Env`
175	- **Invariants:** returns `env` unchanged when it has `name`, `sides`, `reset`, `step` and `render`, and its sides are non-empty with distinct names and disjoint ids
176	- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError` naming every missing behaviour, or `sides` (CORE edge case)
177	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_env/ · **Cases:** a corridor env that passes; an object missing `render`; a two-sided env whose sides share id 0

179	
180	### core.check_agent: Start-up agents and environment match
181	`def check_agent(agents: Agent | Lineup, env: Env) -> Lineup`
182	- **Invariants:** a lone `Agent` on a one-sided environment becomes `{side.name: agent}`; returns a lineup with one agent per side, each of kind `"any"` or matching its side's moves (`Choices` → `"choices"`, `Controls` → `"controls"`)
183	- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` naming each side with no agent, a lone agent on a two-sided environment included (CORE edge case); `MoveKindError` naming both kinds; `ValueError` naming a lineup key that isn't a side
184	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_agent/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the tank; the DQN on the continuous tank; the hunt with only a predator; both sides of the hunt at rung 3
185	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry, crossplay; PL-01

189	### snake.SnakeEnv: Classic Snake
190	`class SnakeEnv(Env): def __init__(self, size: int = 20) -> None; state: SnakeState; name = "snake"; sides = (Side("snake", 11, Choices(3), (0,)),)`
191	- **Invariants:** one id, 0; moves are 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right; observation order: danger straight, left, right; heading up, down, left, right; food up, down, left, right (SNAKE-06); rewards +10, −10 and 0 (SNAKE-07); the game ends after 100 × length steps without food (SNAKE-05); filling the grid ends the game as a win
192	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
193	- **Boundary:** registry, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.SnakeEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 7 with fixed moves to a wall hit; a replay double of a game that eats 2 food
194	- **Used by:** registry, baselines; PL-01

196	### snake.SnakeState: type
197	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class SnakeState: body: tuple[tuple[int, int], ...]; heading: Literal["up", "down", "left", "right"]; food: tuple[int, int] | None; steps_since_food: int; won: bool`
198	- **Invariants:** `body[0]` is the head; cells are `(x, y)` with `0 ≤ x, y < size`; `food` is never on the body, and is `None` only after a win
199	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.SnakeState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 7; a state with the head next to the right wall, heading right

202	## arena
203	
204	### arena.FishState: type
205	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class FishState: x: float; y: float; heading: float; speed: float`
206	- **Invariants:** tank units, origin at the centre, y up; `heading` in degrees anticlockwise from east; `speed` the last step's distance
207	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.FishState/ · **Cases:** a fish at the centre heading east at speed 0.3
208	- **Used by:** tank.TankState, hunt.HuntState
209	
210	### arena.Placement: type
211	`Placement: TypeAlias = tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`
212	- **Invariants:** N fish's positions, float64 N × 2 in tank units, then their headings, float64 N in degrees anticlockwise from east within [0, 360)
213	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.Placement/ · **Cases:** one fish at the centre heading east; 5 fish, one held at the wall
214	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
215	
216	### arena.Readings: type
217	`Readings: TypeAlias = np.ndarray`
218	- **Invariants:** float64 N × rays, every value in [0, 1]; column k is the ray at heading + 360°·k / rays (TANK-07, HUNT-08)
219	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.Readings/ · **Cases:** one fish's 8 wall readings, all 1; 2 fish's 8 circle readings with a ball ahead of the first
220	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
221	
222	### arena.Spots: type
223	`Spots: TypeAlias = np.ndarray`
224	- **Invariants:** float64 n × 2 points in tank units, each within the disc it was drawn over; n = 0 gives shape 0 × 2
225	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.Spots/ · **Cases:** 5 spots on seed 2; no spots
226	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
227	
228	### arena.swim: Turn, then swim to at most the wall
229	`def swim(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, turn: np.ndarray, speed: np.ndarray, *, limit: float) -> Placement`
230	- **Invariants:** for N fish (`pos` N × 2, the others N, angles in degrees), turns each by `turn` first, then moves it `speed` along the new heading, stopping where its centre reaches radius `limit` (the tank's radius less its own) with that heading; returns the new positions and headings and leaves the inputs unchanged (TANK-02, TANK-08, HUNT-02, AD-21)
231	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the lengths disagree
232	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.swim/ · **Cases:** a fish at the centre heading east, turn 0 and speed 0.3; the same fish turning +15° at speed 0; a fish 0.1 from the wall swimming at it at 0.3
233	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
234	
235	### arena.ray_wall: Wall readings
236	`def ray_wall(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, *, radius: float = 10.0, reach: float = 4.0, rays: int = 8) -> Readings`
237	- **Invariants:** N × `rays` readings; ray k points at heading + 360°·k / `rays`; each is the distance along it to the wall circle / `reach`, capped at 1 (TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09)
238	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.ray_wall/ · **Cases:** a fish at the centre heading east (every ray 1); a fish 0.5 from the wall facing it
239	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
240	
241	### arena.ray_circles: Nearest-circle readings
242	`def ray_circles(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, centres: np.ndarray, radii: np.ndarray, *, skip: np.ndarray | None = None, reach: float = 4.0, rays: int = 8) -> Readings`
243	- **Invariants:** N × `rays` readings, rays as `ray_wall`'s: the distance along each ray to the nearest circle's edge / `reach`, 1 when none is within reach and 0 when the ray starts inside one; `skip[i]` is the index in `centres` of fish i itself, or −1, so a fish never sees itself (TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09); M = 0 circles gives all 1
244	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes disagree
245	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.ray_circles/ · **Cases:** a ball's edge 2 straight ahead (forward reading 0.5); a ball 6 away (all 1); two fish on one spot, each skipping itself
246	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
247	
248	### arena.random_spots: Random start spots
249	`def random_spots(rng: np.random.Generator, n: int, *, max_r: float, away: np.ndarray | None = None, min_dist: float = 0.0) -> Spots`
250	- **Invariants:** n × 2 points drawn uniformly over the disc of radius `max_r`, each redrawn until it is at least `min_dist` from every point of `away`; draws only from `rng` (TANK-03, HUNT-01, HUNT-06)
251	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n` < 0, or if 10,000 draws find no spot for one point
252	- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.random_spots/ · **Cases:** 5 spots at least 3 from the centre on seed 2; a pellet respawn at least 0.5 from 5 prey
253	- **Used by:** tank, hunt; PL-01
254	
255	## tank
256	
257	### tank.TankEnv: Fish tank
258	```python
259	class TankEnv(Env):
260	    def __init__(self, fish: int = 5, *, compass: bool = False) -> None: ...
261	    state: TankState; name = "tank"
262	    def reset_to(self, state: TankState) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
263	```
264	- **Invariants:** one side, `Side("fish", 25, …, ids 0..fish-1)`; its moves are `Controls(("turn", "speed"), (-15.0, 0.0), (15.0, 0.3))`, turn in degrees, positive anticlockwise, applied before swimming (TANK-02), or with `compass` `Choices(8)`, 0 north then clockwise every 45°, at speed 0.3 (TWIN-01); movement, wall stop, rays and start spots by `arena` (AD-14); geometry, start, removal, wall stop and time limit follow TANK-01, TANK-03–TANK-06 and TANK-08, a hit on the last step removed first; rewards +1 per step survived and 0 on the removal step, score the mean steps survived (TANK-09)
265	- **Invariants (observation):** ray k points at heading + 45°·k (0 ahead, 1 front-left, …, 7 front-right); entries `3k`, `3k+1`, `3k+2` are its wall, ball-edge and nearest-other-fish distances over the 4-unit reach (1 = nothing within reach); entry 24 is speed / 0.3; the ball's motion isn't observed (TANK-07)
266	- **Invariants (`reset_to`):** starts a game from a hand-placed `state`, as if play had reached it, and returns each living fish's observation; the next `step` continues from `state.step` under the same rules, and the game's generator isn't drawn from (AD-18)
267	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `fish` is outside 1–10; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` naming the fish or field if a fish id is outside `0..fish-1`, a fish lies outside radius 9.7, `survived` lacks a fish or `step` is outside 0–999; otherwise as `core.Env`, the message naming the control and its limits or the fish
268	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 2 with 5 fish and fixed moves until 2 fish are removed on one step; a 1-fish game on seed 7 to the 1,000-step limit; a compass game on seed 2
269	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
270	
271	### tank.TankState: type
272	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TankState: step: int; ball: tuple[float, float]; ball_velocity: tuple[float, float]; fish: dict[int, FishState]; survived: dict[int, int]` with `FishState` as `arena.FishState`
273	- **Invariants:** positions in tank units, origin at the centre, y up, every fish within radius 9.7; `fish` holds the living fish only; `survived` holds every fish's steps survived so far
274	- **Boundary:** tank tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 2 with 5 fish; a ball about to bounce at 30° to the wall
275	- **Used by:** `tank.TankEnv.reset_to`, tank tests
276	
277	## hunt
278	
279	### hunt.HuntEnv: Predator-prey tank
280	```python
281	class HuntEnv(Env):
282	    def __init__(self, prey: int = 5) -> None: ...
283	    state: HuntState; name = "hunt"
284	    def reset_to(self, state: HuntState) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
285	```
286	- **Invariants:** sides `Side("predator", 17, Controls(("turn", "speed"), (-10.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.35)), (0,))` and `Side("prey", 34, the tank's controls, ids 1..prey)` (CORE-03); start per HUNT-01 by `arena.random_spots`; each step in order: every living fish turns then swims by `arena.swim` (predator limit 9.5, prey 9.7; HUNT-02, HUNT-03), the predator catches every prey closer than 0.8 (HUNT-04), each remaining prey within 0.5 of a pellet eats it, the closer prey winning and the lower id on a tie, and the pellet respawns at least 0.5 from every living prey (HUNT-06), then every prey loses energy and one at 0 starves (HUNT-05); ends with no prey left or after 1,000 steps, a removal on the last step counted first (HUNT-07); rewards and scores per HUNT-10 and HUNT-11; energy is an integer in thousandths (AD-21)
287	- **Invariants (observation):** ray k as the tank's; a prey's entries `4k`…`4k+3` are its wall, predator-edge, nearest-other-prey-edge and nearest-pellet-edge distances, 32 is speed / 0.3 and 33 energy (HUNT-08); the predator's `2k` and `2k+1` are its wall and nearest-prey-edge distances, and 16 is speed / 0.35 (HUNT-09)
288	- **Invariants (`reset_to`):** as `tank.TankEnv.reset_to`, from a `HuntState` (AD-18)
289	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `prey` is outside 1–10; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` naming the fish or field if an id is outside the sides, a fish lies outside its limit, an energy is outside 1–1,000, there aren't 10 pellets or `step` is outside 0–999; otherwise as `core.Env`, the message naming the side, the control and its limits, or the fish
290	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.HuntEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 2 with 5 prey and fixed moves until the predator catches one; a replay double of a 1-prey game on seed 7 where the prey never eats and starves at step 500; two prey reaching one pellet on one step
291	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
292	
293	### hunt.HuntState: type
294	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class HuntState: step: int; predator: FishState; prey: dict[int, FishState]; energy: dict[int, int]; pellets: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...]; survived: dict[int, int]; caught: int` with `FishState` as `arena.FishState`
295	- **Invariants:** positions as `tank.TankState`'s, the predator within radius 9.5 and every prey within 9.7; `prey` and `energy` hold the living prey only, energy in thousandths 1–1,000; exactly 10 pellets; `survived` holds every prey's steps survived so far; `caught` is the predator's score
296	- **Boundary:** hunt tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.HuntState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 2 with 5 prey; a prey with energy 500 that ends its move 0.4 from a pellet; a prey 0.7 from the predator after a step
297	- **Used by:** `hunt.HuntEnv.reset_to`, hunt tests

299	## corridor
300	
301	### corridor.CorridorEnv: 1 × 5 test environment
302	`class CorridorEnv(Env): def __init__(self, length: int = 5, max_steps: int = 20) -> None; name = "corridor"`
303	- **Invariants:** one side, `Side("walker", length, Choices(2), (0,))`, starting in cell 0; moves are 0 left, 1 right; reaching the last cell gives +1 and ends the game, and every other step gives 0; `max_steps` also ends the game; the observation is the one-hot position
304	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
305	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/corridor.CorridorEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a 4-step walk right to the end
306	- **Used by:** registry, tests of CORE-05 and CORE-06; PL-01
