full text
The M1 replan and the S3 cut are written as a **Draft**, and they pass the review gate: `dt lint` is clean, `uncovered` and `steps_unplanned` are empty, every task's context packet resolves at 91–812 lines, and `dt ready` lists only M1-T01.

There was nothing to commit. Both files were already committed in `baa7975`, and the working tree was clean before I started.

## What was written
- **`docs/roadmap.md`**: S3 is now split into three milestones, in the same shape as S2:

| Milestone | Pipelines | Depends on | Exit |
|---|---|---|---|
| **M7** Train both sides in the hunt | PL-01 | M2, M4 | Each self-play rung trains on the hunt or the chase; both chase sides learn (CORE-10); Snake's and the tank's records don't change (CORE-09); a 1,000-game self-play run takes ≤ 60 min (QR-01); the hunt training cases (TC-39–49) and the scripted predator and prey cases (TC-50, TC-51) pass |
| **M8** Hunt baselines, curves, replay and cross-play | PL-02, PL-03, PL-05, PL-07 | M5, M7 | The commands run on M7's hunt runs; TC-52–54, TC-59, TC-61 and TC-62 pass |
| **M9** The hunt study | PL-04, PL-06 | M6, M8 | The study runs unattended in ≤ 16 h (≤ 11 h without the taking-turns rung); self-play beats both baselines of each side, and its later generations beat its first (CROSS-04) |

- **`tasks/plan.md` and `tasks/todo.md`**: most M1 tasks needed revising, but none was dropped.
  - **M1-T01** (interface) now also freezes the new core pieces M1 uses: `Side`, `Scores`, `Lineup`, `Match`, `Schedule` and `MissingSideError`. It also freezes `AgentSpec`, `agent_path`, `RunSetup` and `make_run`, plus `PGConfig` and `SelfPlayConfig` as plain types, because `RunConfig` refers to them.
  - **Rewritten:** T02–T08, T10–T14, T18 and T20–T22 now key moves, scores, lineups and results by side, and save each agent as `<side>.pt`.
  - **Added:** M1-T23 builds `make_run` and M1-T24 builds `agent_path`. M1-T14 now waits on both.
  - **Shape:** the critical path is still 7 tasks (T01 → T07 → T15–T18 → T19). The widest wave is now 16 tasks. Checkpoints are unchanged.
  - **Status:** Draft, last updated 2026-10-19, with a dated line in the replan notes.

## Assumptions
- **What M1 builds:** only the one-sided branches. The hunt, chase, schedule, generation and two-sided opponent parts are stubs that name M7. The tank, line and `pg` parts stay with M4.
- **Deferred steps:**
  - The arena geometry steps go to M4.
  - `load_agent` and `RandomAgent` go to M2.
  - The hunt, the chase, the three self-play schedules and the scripted predator and prey go to M7.
- **Opponent order:** the scripted predator and prey are built in M7 rather than M8, because training evaluates against them. M8 scores them.
- **Statistics skill:** null-hypothesis-testing goes on M9, which has the generation comparison. M7 and M8 have no skills or references.
- **Folds:** the "spine is the whole behavior" fold carries over to every new XS task.
- **Base:** I kept the plan's Base at `fadf518`.

## Open questions
- **The checks need extending before M1-T01 can pass.** The import rules in `pyproject.toml` don't mention `rllab.pg` or `rllab.selfplay`, and they allow `rllab.runs` to import only `agent` and `evaluate`. `RunConfig` now refers to both new modules, so `build-setup` has to extend the rules at kickoff. `dt route` can't send a change to build-setup, so I've recorded it in the plan's open questions instead.
- **Interfaces not yet frozen:** the remaining list (arena, tank, hunt, chase, pg, baselines, selfplay, curves, replay, crossplay, study) is frozen by the interface tasks of M2–M9 that first build each one.
- **Open requests:** UR-23/24/57/63/78 stay open until M3, M6 and M7–M9 are planned.

## Uncovered
Nothing is uncovered. Every spec ID and step that M1 doesn't build is deferred with a target:
- **To M2:** BASE-01 and REPLAY-01.
- **To M4:** CORE-07, CORE-09, TANK, TWIN, PG, ABL-06–08 and the arena steps.
- **To M7:** CORE-10, HUNT-01–12, SELF-01–05, SELF-07, BASE-05 and BASE-06.

Do you approve the S3 cut and the revised M1 plan, or want changes? The main choice is the cut itself: M7–M9 mirror S2's three milestones. The alternative would be folding cross-play into the study milestone.
